    # データフレームに変換情報を適用する関数
    def convert_course(row):
        place_code = row["course_type"]  # 競馬場の数値コード
        if place_code in conversion_map_course_type:
            # 直線、カーブ、ゴール前の情報を取得
            course_info = conversion_map_course_type[place_code]
            # 列名を変更
            return pd.Series({
                "goal_range": course_info["最終直線"], 

                "curve": course_info["コーナータイプ"], 
                "goal_slope": course_info["ゴール前坂"],
                "curve_amount": course_info["コーナー数"], 

                "start_point": course_info["スタート位置"], 
                "start_range": course_info["最初直線"], 
                "straight_total": course_info["直線合計"], 
                "curve_total": course_info["コーナー合計m"],
                "curve_R12": course_info["コーナーR12"],
                "curve_R34": course_info["コーナーR34"],
                "height_diff": course_info["高低差"],
                "width": course_info["幅"],
                "start_slope": course_info["最初坂"],
                "flont_slope": course_info["向正面坂"],
                "first_curve_slope": course_info["最初コーナー坂"],
                "last_curve_slope": course_info["最終コーナー坂"],

                # 新たに追加された項目（すべてcourse_infoから取得）
                "waku_1": course_info["waku_1"],
                "waku_2": course_info["waku_2"],
                "waku_3": course_info["waku_3"],
                "waku_4": course_info["waku_4"],
                "waku_5": course_info["waku_5"],
                "waku_6": course_info["waku_6"],
                "waku_7": course_info["waku_7"],
                "waku_8": course_info["waku_8"],

                "position_1": course_info["position_1"],
                "position_2": course_info["position_2"],
                "position_3": course_info["position_3"],
                "position_4": course_info["position_4"],

                "course_len_diff_-500": course_info["course_len_diff_-500"],
                "course_len_diff_-350": course_info["course_len_diff_-350"],
                "course_len_diff_-150": course_info["course_len_diff_-150"],
                "course_len_diff_0": course_info["course_len_diff_0"],
                "course_len_diff_150": course_info["course_len_diff_150"],
                "course_len_diff_350": course_info["course_len_diff_350"],
                "course_len_diff_500": course_info["course_len_diff_500"],

                "class_diff_up": course_info["class_diff_up"],
                "class_diff_stay": course_info["class_diff_stay"],
                "class_diff_down": course_info["class_diff_down"],

                "age_3": course_info["age_3"],
                "age_4": course_info["age_4"],
                "age_5": course_info["age_5"],
                "age_6": course_info["age_6"],

                "sex_men": course_info["sex_men"],
                "sex_women": course_info["sex_women"],
                "sex_nosex": course_info["sex_nosex"],
                # 追加列（_EX）
                "waku_1_EX": course_info["waku_1_EX"], "waku_2_EX": course_info["waku_2_EX"], "waku_3_EX": course_info["waku_3_EX"], "waku_4_EX": course_info["waku_4_EX"],
                "waku_5_EX": course_info["waku_5_EX"], "waku_6_EX": course_info["waku_6_EX"], "waku_7_EX": course_info["waku_7_EX"], "waku_8_EX": course_info["waku_8_EX"],
                "position_1_EX": course_info["position_1_EX"], "position_2_EX": course_info["position_2_EX"], "position_3_EX": course_info["position_3_EX"], "position_4_EX": course_info["position_4_EX"],
                "course_len_diff_-500_EX": course_info["course_len_diff_-500_EX"], "course_len_diff_-350_EX": course_info["course_len_diff_-350_EX"], "course_len_diff_-150_EX": course_info["course_len_diff_-150_EX"],
                "course_len_diff_0_EX": course_info["course_len_diff_0_EX"], "course_len_diff_150_EX": course_info["course_len_diff_150_EX"], "course_len_diff_350_EX": course_info["course_len_diff_350_EX"], "course_len_diff_500_EX": course_info["course_len_diff_500_EX"],
                "class_diff_up_EX": course_info["class_diff_up_EX"], "class_diff_stay_EX": course_info["class_diff_stay_EX"], "class_diff_down_EX": course_info["class_diff_down_EX"],
                "age_3_EX": course_info["age_3_EX"], "age_4_EX": course_info["age_4_EX"], "age_5_EX": course_info["age_5_EX"], "age_6_EX": course_info["age_6_EX"],
                "sex_men_EX": course_info["sex_men_EX"], "sex_women_EX": course_info["sex_women_EX"], "sex_nosex_EX": course_info["sex_nosex_EX"],
            })
        else:
            # 値が無い場合はすべてNoneまたは0（数値列）で埋める
            return pd.Series({col: None for col in [
                "goal_range", "curve", "goal_slope", "curve_amount", "start_point", "start_range", "straight_total",
                "curve_total", "curve_R12", "curve_R34", "height_diff", "width", "start_slope", "flont_slope",
                "first_curve_slope", "last_curve_slope",

                "waku_1", "waku_2", "waku_3", "waku_4", "waku_5", "waku_6", "waku_7", "waku_8",
                "position_1", "position_2", "position_3", "position_4",
                "course_len_diff_-500", "course_len_diff_-350", "course_len_diff_-150",
                "course_len_diff_0", "course_len_diff_150", "course_len_diff_350", "course_len_diff_500",
                "class_diff_up", "class_diff_stay", "class_diff_down",
                "age_3", "age_4", "age_5", "age_6",
                "sex_men", "sex_women", "sex_nosex","waku_1_EX", "waku_2_EX", "waku_3_EX", "waku_4_EX", "waku_5_EX", "waku_6_EX", "waku_7_EX", "waku_8_EX",
                "position_1_EX", "position_2_EX", "position_3_EX", "position_4_EX",
                "course_len_diff_-500_EX", "course_len_diff_-350_EX", "course_len_diff_-150_EX", "course_len_diff_0_EX", "course_len_diff_150_EX", "course_len_diff_350_EX", "course_len_diff_500_EX",
                "class_diff_up_EX", "class_diff_stay_EX", "class_diff_down_EX",
                "age_3_EX", "age_4_EX", "age_5_EX", "age_6_EX",
                "sex_men_EX", "sex_women_EX", "sex_nosex_EX"
            ]})

    # 適用：DataFrameに新しい列を一括で追加
    df[
        ['goal_range', 'curve', 'goal_slope', 'curve_amount', 'start_point', 'start_range', 'straight_total',
        'curve_total', 'curve_R12', 'curve_R34', 'height_diff', 'width', 'start_slope', 'flont_slope',
        'first_curve_slope', 'last_curve_slope',

        'waku_1', 'waku_2', 'waku_3', 'waku_4', 'waku_5', 'waku_6', 'waku_7', 'waku_8',
        'position_1', 'position_2', 'position_3', 'position_4',
        'course_len_diff_-500', 'course_len_diff_-350', 'course_len_diff_-150',
        'course_len_diff_0', 'course_len_diff_150', 'course_len_diff_350', 'course_len_diff_500',
        'class_diff_up', 'class_diff_stay', 'class_diff_down',
        'age_3', 'age_4', 'age_5', 'age_6',
        'sex_men', 'sex_women', 'sex_nosex',"waku_1_EX", "waku_2_EX", "waku_3_EX", "waku_4_EX", "waku_5_EX", "waku_6_EX", "waku_7_EX", "waku_8_EX",
        "position_1_EX", "position_2_EX", "position_3_EX", "position_4_EX",
        "course_len_diff_-500_EX", "course_len_diff_-350_EX", "course_len_diff_-150_EX", "course_len_diff_0_EX", "course_len_diff_150_EX", "course_len_diff_350_EX", "course_len_diff_500_EX",
        "class_diff_up_EX", "class_diff_stay_EX", "class_diff_down_EX",
        "age_3_EX", "age_4_EX", "age_5_EX", "age_6_EX",
        "sex_men_EX", "sex_women_EX", "sex_nosex_EX"
        ]
    ] = df.apply(convert_course, axis=1)
    df["goal_range_100"] = df["goal_range"].astype(float)/100
